#### What this PR does / why we need it:
Adds `GroupNameAnnotation` and `PrebuiltWorkloadAnnotation` pod annotations, available when the `WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations` feature gate is enabled. 

Without this gate, Kueue stores the pod group name as a pod label `kueue.x-k8s.io/pod-group-name`, which Kubernetes limits to 63 characters. 

This causes LWS with names longer than 39 characters to fail at pod creation, since the workload name is formatted as `leaderworkerset-<name>-<groupIndex>-<hash5>` (24 chars of fixed overhead), so a 39-char name produces a 63-char label value. 

Moving the group name to an annotation removes that constraint and raises the effective limit to 50 characters (with workers) or 52 characters (leader-only).

Effective name limits
| LWS name length | Size | WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations | Pods created                                                   |
|-----------------|------|-------------------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------|
| 39              | 1    | disabled                      | ✅ `pod-group-name` label value fits 63 chars                   |
| 39              | 3    | disabled                      | ✅ `pod-group-name` label value fits 63 chars                   |
| 40              | any  | disabled                      | ❌ `pod-group-name` label value exceeds 63 chars                |
| 52              | 1    | enabled                       | ✅ leader STS `controller-revision-hash` label fits 63 chars    |
| 53              | 1    | enabled                       | ❌ leader STS `controller-revision-hash` label exceeds 63 chars |
| 50              | 3    | enabled                       | ✅ worker STS `controller-revision-hash` label fits 63 chars    |
| 51              | 3    | enabled                       | ❌ worker STS `controller-revision-hash` label exceeds 63 chars |


Added e2e tests in `test/e2e/sequential/extended/` that verify the boundary behaviour with the feature gate on and off, using Size(3) (1 leader + 2 workers) to exercise the worker StatefulSet constraint.

With the feature gate enabled, the new ceiling comes from StatefulSet naming constraints, which the [LWS documentation covers](https://lws.sigs.k8s.io/docs/troubleshooting/#3-unable-to-create-lws-object-with-a-name-exceeding-51-characters
):

> This issue occurs because StatefulSet names exceeding 57 characters prevent pods from starting, as described in [this Kubernetes issue](https://github.com/kubernetes/kubernetes/issues/64023). Since LWS relies on StatefulSets, it is not possible to create an LWS object with a name longer than 51 characters.

#### Which issue(s) this PR fixes:

#### Special notes for your reviewer:

#### Does this PR introduce a user-facing change?
<!--
If no, just write "NONE" in the release-note block below.
If yes, a release note is required:
Enter your extended release note in the block below. If the PR requires additional action from users switching to the new release, include the string "action required".
-->
```release-note
Introduced the `WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations` feature gate. When enabled, Kueue stores pod-group and prebuilt-workload names on the annotations (`kueue.x-k8s.io/pod-group-name` and `kueue.x-k8s.io/prebuilt-workload-name`). The labels are still read as a fallback if the annotations are absent, and with the gate disabled the labels continue to be used for both reading and writing.
```

  It will definitely simplify some occurrences when we don't use bool value, so we can use this func inline.
  For other cases, It's nice to have human readable `hasPrebuiltWorkload`, alternative `prebuiltWorkload != ""` isn’t hard to read either.
  
  For me it would make sense to simplify, it will make inline usage possible and caller wouldn't need to inspect `PrebuiltWorkloadFor` to understand what the boolean flag means.

```diff
+// GetPodGroupName returns the pod group name for the given pod. It reads the

+func GetPodGroupName(p *corev1.Pod) (string, bool) {
+	if features.Enabled(features.WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations) {

+	if features.Enabled(features.WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations) {
```

```diff
+	if features.Enabled(features.WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations) {
+		if name := p.Annotations[podconstants.GroupNameAnnotation]; name != "" {
```
```diff
+			return name
+		}
+	}
```
```diff
+	return p.Labels[podconstants.GroupNameLabel]
+}
+
+// SetPodGroupName stores the pod group name on the given pod.
+// When the WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations feature gate is enabled
+// the name is written to the GroupNameAnnotation. Otherwise,
+// it is written to the GroupNameLabel.
```

```diff
+func GetPodGroupName(p *corev1.Pod) string {

+// SetPodGroupName stores the pod group name on the given pod.

+// it is written to the GroupNameLabel.
+func SetPodGroupName(p *corev1.Pod, groupName string) {
+	if features.Enabled(features.WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations) {
+		delete(p.Labels, podconstants.GroupNameLabel)
+		if p.Annotations == nil {
+			p.Annotations = make(map[string]string, 1)
+		}
+		p.Annotations[podconstants.GroupNameAnnotation] = groupName
+	} else {
+		if p.Labels == nil {
+			p.Labels = make(map[string]string, 1)
+		}
+		p.Labels[podconstants.GroupNameLabel] = groupName
+	}
```

```diff
+
```

```diff
+
```

```diff
+		util.ExpectObjectToBeDeleted(ctx, k8sClient, cq, true)

+

+					if cfg.FeatureGates == nil {
+						cfg.FeatureGates = make(map[string]bool)
+					}
+					cfg.FeatureGates[string(features.WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations)] = false
+				})
+			}
+		})
+

+				Size(3).
+				Replicas(1).
+				RequestAndLimit(corev1.ResourceCPU, "200m").

+				Queue(lq.Name).

+					g.Expect(createdLeaderWorkerSet.Status.ReadyReplicas).To(gomega.Equal(int32(1)))
+					g.Expect(createdLeaderWorkerSet.Status.Conditions).To(utiltesting.HaveConditionStatusTrueAndReason("Available", "AllGroupsReady"))
+				}, util.LongTimeout, util.Interval).Should(gomega.Succeed())

+

+					}, client.InNamespace(lws.Namespace))).Should(gomega.Succeed())
+					g.Expect(pods.Items).To(gomega.BeEmpty())
+				}, util.MediumTimeout, util.Interval).Should(gomega.Succeed())
+			})
+
+			ginkgo.By("check workload is deleted", func() {

+
+		ginkgo.It("should fail to admit group with 40-character lws name, pod group name label exceeds 63 chars", func() {

+
```

```diff
+					util.SetPodsPhase(ctx, k8sClient, corev1.PodSucceeded, pod2)

+						g.Expect(wl.Status.Conditions).To(utiltesting.HaveConditionStatusTrue(kueue.WorkloadFinished))

+				})
+
+				ginkgo.By("checking pod and workload finalizers are removed", func() {

+					util.ExpectWorkloadsFinalizedOrGone(ctx, k8sClient, wlLookupKey)
```

### Review by [@ivnovakov](https://github.com/ivnovakov) (commented) — 2026-05-07T13:35:05Z

- **pkg/controller/jobs/pod/pod_multikueue_adapter.go:75** by [@ivnovakov](https://github.com/ivnovakov) — 2026-05-07T13:35:05Z
```diff
@@ -66,20 +63,22 @@ func (b *multiKueueAdapter) DeleteRemoteObject(ctx context.Context, remoteClient
 		return client.IgnoreNotFound(err)
 	}
 
-	if !isPodAPartOfGroup(pod) {
+	groupName := GetPodGroupName(&pod)
+	if groupName == "" {
 		return client.IgnoreNotFound(remoteClient.Delete(ctx, &pod))
 	}
 
-	groupName := podGroupName(pod)
 	podGroup := &corev1.PodList{}
-	err = remoteClient.List(ctx, podGroup, client.InNamespace(key.Namespace), client.MatchingLabels{podconstants.GroupNameLabel: groupName})
```

  `remoteClient` has no local cache, so MatchingFields with a custom field index key fails against it. 
  I needed to propagate `localClient` to `DeleteRemoteObject()` to use the manager's indexed client. 
  Updated `MultiKueueAdapter` interface and all implementations.
  Tests are passing now.


  Yes, we need this. Without it, the job reconciler's `jobframework.EquivalentToWorkload` calls `equality.ComparePodSetSlices` which will fail because the workload's `PodSet` containers won't have `TerminationMessagePath`, `TerminationMessagePolicy`, and `ImagePullPolicy` set, so the reconciler will skip prebuilt workload adoption.
  
  The same container was used for both job and workload because `MakeJob` explicitly initializes `Resources` with empty maps:
  ```go
  Resources: corev1.ResourceRequirements{Requests: corev1.ResourceList{}, Limits: corev1.ResourceList{}}
  ```
  which would not match a nil `Resources` in the workload and cause the same mismatch.

  Yes, we need to reject it, because there are original tests that check this behavior.
  - "prebuilt workload for pod group valid" (ValidateCreate):
    https://github.com/epam/kubernetes-kueue/blob/bug/long-workload-name-fails/pkg/controller/jobs/pod/pod_webhook_test.go 
  - "prebuilt workload for pod group invalid" (ValidateCreate):                                                                                                                      
    https://github.com/epam/kubernetes-kueue/blob/bug/long-workload-name-fails/pkg/controller/jobs/pod/pod_webhook_test.go                                                             
  - "prebuilt workload for pod group invalid" (ValidateUpdate):
    https://github.com/epam/kubernetes-kueue/blob/bug/long-workload-name-fails/pkg/controller/jobs/pod/pod_webhook_test.go

### Review by [@mbobrovskyi](https://github.com/mbobrovskyi) (commented) — 2026-05-07T19:22:23Z

- **pkg/controller/jobs/pod/pod_multikueue_adapter.go:75** by [@mbobrovskyi](https://github.com/mbobrovskyi) — 2026-05-07T19:22:23Z
```diff
@@ -66,20 +63,22 @@ func (b *multiKueueAdapter) DeleteRemoteObject(ctx context.Context, remoteClient
 		return client.IgnoreNotFound(err)
 	}
 
-	if !isPodAPartOfGroup(pod) {
+	groupName := GetPodGroupName(&pod)
+	if groupName == "" {
 		return client.IgnoreNotFound(remoteClient.Delete(ctx, &pod))
 	}
 
-	groupName := podGroupName(pod)
 	podGroup := &corev1.PodList{}
-	err = remoteClient.List(ctx, podGroup, client.InNamespace(key.Namespace), client.MatchingLabels{podconstants.GroupNameLabel: groupName})
```

  > remoteClient has no local cache, so MatchingFields with a custom field index key fails against it.
  I needed to propagate localClient to DeleteRemoteObject() to use the manager's indexed client.
  Updated MultiKueueAdapter interface and all implementations.
  Tests are passing now.
  
  I don’t think this will work, because it’s possible that the remote client has Pods while the local one does not. In that case, we would lose information about those dummy Pods.

```diff
+
```
```diff
+var _ = ginkgo.Describe("WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations", ginkgo.Ordered, ginkgo.ContinueOnFailure, ginkgo.Label("feature:workloadidentifierannotations"), func() {
+	var (
+		ns *corev1.Namespace
+		rf *kueue.ResourceFlavor
+		cq *kueue.ClusterQueue
+		lq *kueue.LocalQueue
+	)
+
+	ginkgo.BeforeEach(func() {
+		ns = util.CreateNamespaceFromPrefixWithLog(ctx, k8sClient, "lws-e2e-")
+
+		rf = utiltestingapi.MakeResourceFlavor("rf-"+ns.Name).NodeLabel("instance-type", "on-demand").Obj()
+		util.MustCreate(ctx, k8sClient, rf)
+
+		cq = utiltestingapi.MakeClusterQueue("cq-" + ns.Name).
+			ResourceGroup(
+				*utiltestingapi.MakeFlavorQuotas(rf.Name).
+					Resource(corev1.ResourceCPU, "5").
+					Obj(),
+			).
+			Obj()
+		util.CreateClusterQueuesAndWaitForActive(ctx, k8sClient, cq)
+
+		lq = utiltestingapi.MakeLocalQueue("lq-"+ns.Name, ns.Name).ClusterQueue(cq.Name).Obj()
+		util.CreateLocalQueuesAndWaitForActive(ctx, k8sClient, lq)
+	})
+
+	ginkgo.AfterEach(func() {
+		gomega.Expect(util.DeleteAllLeaderWorkerSetsInNamespace(ctx, k8sClient, ns)).To(gomega.Succeed())
+		gomega.Expect(util.DeleteNamespace(ctx, k8sClient, ns)).To(gomega.Succeed())
+		util.ExpectObjectToBeDeleted(ctx, k8sClient, cq, true)
+		util.ExpectObjectToBeDeleted(ctx, k8sClient, rf, true)
+		util.ExpectAllPodsInNamespaceDeleted(ctx, k8sClient, ns)
+	})
+
+	ginkgo.Context("with WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations disabled", func() {
+		ginkgo.BeforeAll(func() {
+			if defaultKueueCfg.FeatureGates[string(features.WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations)] != false {
+				util.UpdateKueueConfigurationAndRestart(ctx, k8sClient, defaultKueueCfg, kindClusterName, func(cfg *config.Configuration) {
+					if cfg.FeatureGates == nil {
+						cfg.FeatureGates = make(map[string]bool, 1)
+					}
+					cfg.FeatureGates[string(features.WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations)] = false
+				})
+			}
+		})
+
+		ginkgo.It("should admit group with 39-character lws name, pod group name label is 63 chars", func() {
+			lwsName := strings.Repeat("a", 39)
+			lws := leaderworkersettesting.MakeLeaderWorkerSet(lwsName, ns.Name).
+				Image(util.GetAgnHostImage(), util.BehaviorWaitForDeletion).
+				Size(3).
+				Replicas(1).
+				RequestAndLimit(corev1.ResourceCPU, "200m").
+				TerminationGracePeriod(1).
+				Queue(lq.Name).
+				Obj()
+
+			ginkgo.By("create a LeaderWorkerSet", func() {
+				util.MustCreate(ctx, k8sClient, lws)
+			})
+
+			ginkgo.By("waiting for replicas to be ready", func() {
+				createdLeaderWorkerSet := &leaderworkersetv1.LeaderWorkerSet{}
+				gomega.Eventually(func(g gomega.Gomega) {
+					g.Expect(k8sClient.Get(ctx, client.ObjectKeyFromObject(lws), createdLeaderWorkerSet)).To(gomega.Succeed())
+					g.Expect(createdLeaderWorkerSet.Status.ReadyReplicas).To(gomega.Equal(int32(1)))
+					g.Expect(createdLeaderWorkerSet.Status.Conditions).To(utiltesting.HaveConditionStatusTrueAndReason("Available", "AllGroupsReady"))
+				}, util.LongTimeout, util.Interval).Should(gomega.Succeed())
+			})
+
+			ginkgo.By("checking pods have group name as label, not annotation", func() {
+				pods := &corev1.PodList{}
+				gomega.Eventually(func(g gomega.Gomega) {
+					g.Expect(k8sClient.List(ctx, pods, client.MatchingLabels{
+						leaderworkersetv1.SetNameLabelKey: lws.Name,
+					}, client.InNamespace(ns.Name))).To(gomega.Succeed())
+					g.Expect(pods.Items).ToNot(gomega.BeEmpty())
+					for _, pod := range pods.Items {
+						g.Expect(pod.Labels).To(gomega.HaveKey(podconstants.GroupNameLabel))
+						g.Expect(pod.Annotations).ToNot(gomega.HaveKey(podconstants.GroupNameAnnotation))
+					}
+				}, util.LongTimeout, util.Interval).Should(gomega.Succeed())
+			})
+		})
```

```diff
+  description: |

+  note: |
+    This annotation is allowed only when the WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations feature is enabled.
+    When enabled, we use the value of Pod group name from the annotation by default;
+    if it is not present, we fall back to the label.
```

```diff
     The label key indicates the name of the group of Pods that should be admitted together.
+  note: |
+    This label is always allowed, including when the WorkloadIdentifierAnnotations feature is enabled.
+    When the feature is enabled, we use the annotation value by default;
+    if the annotation is absent, we fall back to this label.

+  type: Annotation
```

```diff
+					g.Expect(createdLeaderWorkerSet.Status.Conditions).To(utiltesting.HaveConditionStatusTrueAndReason("Available", "AllGroupsReady"))
```
